# Skills · H · part 17

845 entries, harness-tool-linking to hasdata-shopping. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **harness-tool-linking**: Detect which AI tool the user runs and write the matching ai-harness adapter file(s). Claude Code, Codex, Pi, GitHub Copilot CLI, and Google Antigravity CLI are fully automated; other tools are recognized and given a manual snippet. Used by /sth-harness:init and the Codex/Pi/Copilot CLI/AGY sth-harness skill. ([danielemiliogarcia/sth-harness-plugin](https://github.com/danielemiliogarcia/sth-harness-plugin/tree/HEAD/skills/harness-tool-linking) · ★1)
